Page1-2

Bismillahir Rahmanin Rahim

Lailah na saman kujera tana kallon wani series na Indian mai Suna (kaddaran Rayuwa) sai kanwanta husna ta shigo tace Aunty lailah, tace na'am mai yafaru sai husna tace yaya Abba nakiranki kice mishi bana nan tor Aunty sai husna tafita tace batanan, yaya Abba yace to inta dawo kice mata nazo tor yaya sai ta koma ciki tagaya mata abun da yace sai ta ja tsaki ta dauke kai, mama da ta fito daga kitchen ne ta hauta da fadan akan me zatace batanan sai tafara mata fada daga baya ta sassauto tace lailah,na'am mama mai yasa kike ma Abba irin Wannan abun bai dace ba samsam wulakanci bai da kyau wallahi amman yanzu inya dawo Dan Allah ki amsa mishi saboda naga duk san da yazo sai kin bada uzuri haba lailah ki gyra Dan Allah, mama wlh bana son yaya Abba mama sai ta fashe da matsanancin kuka ta shiga daki tana kuka sossai kusan minti sha biyar ta dauka tana kuka kafun barci yayi nasaran dauke ta...... 

Mama kuma na daki tana tunanin yanda zata bullo ma alamarin nan da ya shamata kai ta kamo Wannan ta daura ta kamo wanchan ta kwance tananan zaune ta kuma jin sallaman Abba , takira husna tace take ta duba Wanda ke sallama tace to tajuya taje ta bude kofan taga yaya Abba ne ta gaishe shi tace ya shigo yace a'a kawai takira mai lailah tace to tajuya taje kiran ta, Aunty lailah yaya Abba na kiranki tayi kaman bats jita bahh husna ta kara maimaitawa Aunty Ana kiranki k Dan Allah wuce naji taja tsaki husna tafita tana murguda Bali taje tace mishi tana zuwa yaya yace to kanwata nagode murmushi kawai husna tayi ta koma ciki sai da laila tagama jan aji da bata lokaci kafun tafito, tana fitowa tamai sallama ta gaishe shi ya amsamata da kulawa daga haka batace komai ba taja bakinta tayi shuru ta daure fuska "uwa uba taga mutuwanta " yace lailah me laifina dannace ina sonki karkimanta masoyi yafi makiyi kitausayamun lailah zuciyata na azabtuwa da soyayyarki Dan Allah ki soni ko kadan ne my princess, tadago ta kalleshi ta dauke kai batace komai ba yace talk to me pls my princess I don't like your silent, ta juyo tace mezance ma Dan Allah nace maka bana sanka ni ba soyayya bane a gabana karatu nake yanzu ka fahimce ni Dan Allah tafashe da masananci kuka harda majina tana shasheka zuba mata ido yayi yana kallonta a zuciyar ta ko cewa tayi kayi hakuri yaya Abba babu macen dazata ganka takika kanada kyau kudi ga hankali da natsuwa amma kash bazan iya sonka ba saboda zuciyata tadade da barina tana tare da wani shi kadai ne a ciki bazan iya San wanin sa ba kayi hakuri yayana sai yakatse ta daga tunanin da take Dan Allah kiyi hakuri ki daina kukannan pls kayi hakuri zan jiraki harki kammala indai Wannan zai saki farin ciki is that Okh dagowa tayi tana murmushi tana hawaye ta kada mishi kai yace to share hawayan bar kuka kinji my princess tasa hannu ta share yafara janta da hira tuntana shuru hartafara bashi amsa hira ya barke a tsakanin su suna cikin hiran sukaji kiran sallan magrib yace sai gobe my princess tace Allah ya tashemu ya amsa da Amen ya wuce ita kuma tashiga ciki taje tayi alwala tayi sallah bayan ta idar tayi azkar na yamma bayan ta gama tafito parlor inda tasamu daddyta ya dawo ta duka har kasa ta gaishe shi ya amsa da fara'a sossai yace yar daddy yakike ya karatu tace lfy lau daddy ya aiki daddy yace alhamdulillah daughter sai sukafara hira saiga mama da husna sun sauko husna ta gaishe da daddy, daddy ya amsa tare da cewa autana ykk ya karatu tace lfy lau daddy ya aiki daddy yace Alhamdulillah autana mama kuma tamai sannu da dawowa daddy ya amsa tare dacewa yagida da yara mama tace lfy lau Alhaji ya aiki Daddy yace Alhamdulillah maman yara murmushi kawai mama tayi daga haka sukafara hira har akayi kiran sallan isha daddy yaje masallaci mama kuma taja yaranta sukaje sukayi nasu bayan duk sun idar suka sauko jiran dawowan daddy a dining area after 5min daddy ya shigo yazo yasamesu a dining area yaja kujera yazauna sai lailah ta tashi tayi serving kowa bayan sun kammala duk suka koma parlor Suna kallo daddy kuma na aiki a laptop har 10 kafun duk sutashi su je su kwanta mama taje dakinta ta shirya kafun taje turaka a bangaren lailah kuma bayan tayi wanka tayi alwala ta kwanta tafara aikinta na kullun wato tunani baita tasamu yin barci bahh sai 1:00am barci ya dauketa Asuba tagari.......

page 3-4

Washe gari taka sance Monday  Ana hada hadan zuwa school husna tana ss2 while lailah ma na shirin   makaranta Dan tana lectures ranan kuma na safane, sauri take  Dan kartayi latti malamin da suke dashi bai daukan raini inya rigaka shiga lecture hall to bazaka shiga ba kuma ba'a surutu ajin shi, lailah na gamawa taje dakin mama ta anso kudi tayi mata sallama taje wurin driver ya kaita school acikin 20min suka isa saboda gudun da driver yayi Dan takusa latti yana ajeta yajuya ita kuma tayi tsauri ta shige lecture hall tana shiga da 2min sai ga lectural din su ya shigo dr sa'ad muhammad Aliyu kenan dan matashi mai jida kudi,aji, kyau, nasaba, miskiline na karshe, miskiline na bugawa a jarida ba'a gane gaban shi balle bayan shi mommy ne kawai ke iyawa dashi itama sai tayi da gaske wani sa'in. 
  
Yawancin student din makarantar na mutuwar son shi wasu na gayamai ko kallo basu ishe shiba wasuko sun bar ma zuciya kaman su ooo W kira suna bahh... Wasu miskilan cin shine ke burgesu wasu dan kyawun shi suke san shi. 

Sallama yayi kafun ya shiga suka amsa mai hadi da gaisuwa ya amsa sannan yafara lecture in yana lecture ba'a surutu, ba'a tanbaya har sai yagama kuma daya riga ka shiga hall tor ka hakura kawai dan inka shiga ka/kin siya/siyo ma kanka/kanki disgrace( wulakanci). 

Yana gama lecture ya fara tanbaya as usual, yace k sai lailah ta juya tana neman inda k yake, yadaka mata tsawa yace dake nake tuni jikinta tafara kyarma ta tashi ya jeho mata tanbaya tayi shuru tana tunani sai yace bada k nake bahh tace nine sir sai tafara bashi amsa tana gamawa aka tafa mata tazauna tana ajiyar zuciy, (a zuciyarta tace Allah nagode ma daka bani ikon amsawa) ya tan bayi wasu student kafun time din shi yayi lokacin fitan shi nayi yafita wani lectural ya shiga basu suka gama lectures ba sai 6pm suka kare tana fita daga lecture hall ta hango driver har yazo taje ta shiga yaja mota sai gida su na kaiwa gida ta bude kofan ta fita, tana shiga parlor taga ba kowa sai ta leka kitchen taga mama ita kadai dan husna na islamiya bata dawo ba, ta gaida mama tamata sannu da gida kafun ta wuce dakinta rana shiga ta ajiye jakarta ta cire hijab da kayanta ta daura towel ta shiga wanka mintin ta 10 a bayj ta fito ta sa inner wears dinta sannan tasa dogon riga ko mai bata shafa ba taje ta samu mama a kitchen ta kama mata aiki suna kammalawa ana
Kiran magarib mama ta haura sama tajeyin sallah lailah kuma ta tsaya jera coolers a dining area tana gama wa itama je yin sallah bayan ta idar tagama azkar dinta ta sauko ta tadda husna ne kawai a parlor  tana kallo itama tazauna suka cigaba da kallon suna hira jefi jefi     suna nan zaune akayi kiran sallah isha'i sai ga daddy ya fito daga part dinshi za shi masallaci duk suka gaishe shi ya amsa ya wuce masallaci dan anfara tayarwa ba daddy ya dawo ba sai 8:30pm ya  shiga parlor da sallama duk suka amsa hadi dayi mai sannu dazuwa, mama ajiye littafin dake hannunta tayi tace duk suje dining dama daddy yayi gaba abun shi su husna ne suka taso, husna tayi serving dinsu dukaci sukayi hamdala husna da lailah suka kwashe kwanoni mama da daddy sukayi parlor, husna ta tsaya wankesu lailah kuma ta koma parlor tayi masu daddy sai da safe akan zataje tayi karatu suna da test gobe suka ce tor Allah yabada sa'a lailah ta amsa da Ameen kafun ta juya ta wuce dakinta  tana shiga ta dauko littafin dazata karanta tafara baita tagama ba sai 2 shima dan barci ya ci karfinta ne tanema gurin kwanciya bayan tayi addu'a..... 

Washe gari lecture din afternoon suke dashi 2:00 kuma test dinne first, tana gama aikin da zatayi tazo tacigaba da karatun ta sai wurin 12:30 ta tashi ta shiga wanka baita tafito ba sai 12:50 tana fitowa tayi shafe shafenta tasa kaya da hijab ta tayar da sallah tana idarwa tayi addu'a  ta mike taje ta dauko abincinta taci tana gamawa ta tsuri jakarta tarike handout dinta a hannu ta fito a parlor tasami mama tayi ma mama sallama har tafara tafiya mama ta ce taje taduba dressing mirro akwai 1000 ta dauka tace to mama ta juya ta je dakin mama ta dauko ta dawo tayi mata sallama tafita minti 20 ya kaisu saboda go slow dake hanyya 1:50 suka kai school har kofan hall ya ajiyeta ta sauka ta shiga ciki tanemu wuri ta zauna tafara maraja'a kafin malamin ya shigo 2 nayi ya shigo yana shiga yafara dube dube har yahango abinda yake nema yana ganinta ya sauke ajiyar zuciya a boye sai yace yanzu zamu  fara test are u all ready suka amsa da yes sir yace okh ya juya ya rubuta questions din yace a fara the test is for 30min duk sukafara shi kuma yana zagaya ajin yana duba student  har yakai kan lailah ya tsaya kallon abin da take ta amsa 3 saura daya dayake 4 questions ne yana tsayawa akanta ta bar rubutun yace tacigaba taki sai yayi murmushi yayi gaba. 

bayan angama test din yace lailah ta kawo office dinshi tace to sir ya fita ta bishi da shi a baya  en aji suka fara gulma balle matan dama malamin badai kyau bah yanada fara'a sossai ga surutu amma ba sossai ba, suna isa office dinshi yabata hayya ta shiga ya bita a baya ta ajiye books din ta juya zata koma yakirata khadija ta amsa da na'am yace i want to talk to u tace  okh sir ya bata izinin zama ta  zauna.......

Yace khadija tace na'am yace a gaskiya na yaba da hankalinki natsuwa dakuma tarbiya khadija bazan boye miki ba tun randa nafara ganinki na kamu da sonki khadija ba laifina bane Allah ne ya daura mun sonki kuma ba chutarki zanyi ba da aure nake sanki wlh, dan Allah ki bani dama khadija, wani banzan kallo take binshi dashi kafun ta dauke kai yace answer me pls khadija tamai banza yace kona baki lokaci kai tsaye tace A a zuciyar ta ko cewa tayi wlh aikin banza kake dan tuni naba wani zuciyata in ma zaka hakura ka hakura... Yace mekike tunani ne haka khadija nayi ta kiran sunan ki baki jiba sai dana buga desk ni bance kiyi tunanin answer yanzu ba kibari sai kinje gida kin huta tace tor nagode, u can go ta tashi ta fita tana fita tace zakayi ka bari kai ma dan inada wani dan nachi a gida daganan ta wuce cafeteria tana gamawa taje tayi sallan la'asar data idar tayi addu'ar ta tagama ta gashi ta wuce hall Dan suna da lecture 4:30 bash zasu fito ba sai 6, 6 nayi aka gama lecture ta fito bata sami driver ba ta tsaya jiran shi sai ga lectural Muhahd Alameen yace tazo ta shiga ya kaita kaita gida tace no nagode sir am greatful Ina jiran driver ne yanzu zaizo nagode yace no ki shigo kawai na ajiyeki magriba ya kusa is getting late, a zuciyarta ko Allah Allah take driver yazo Dan wanna surutun ya isheta, yana rufe baking shi sai ga driver ya shigo ta sauke ajiyar zuciya hadi da gode ma Allah da yasa driver yazo a lokacin, tace ga driver nan ma yazo nagode sai gobe tawuce ta barshi nan binta ya ke da kallo har ta shige mota driver yaja sai da suka fita a gate kafun hankalin shi ya dawo ya sauke ajiyar zuciya yace Oh! Allah kasa ma wannan bai warka sona yana gama fadi yaja motar shi ya bar makarantar. 

basu suka kai ba sai 7:00 saboda holdup din da ke hanyya tana fita direct parlor ta shiga babu kowa ta haura ta shiga dakin mama ta tarar tana sallah ta juya ta ja kofar kamun ta shiga dakinta ta shiga na husna suka gaisa ta wuce nata dakin tana shiga ta tube ta daura towel ta shiga wanka bayan 10min ta fito tana fita tasa dogon riga da hijab ta shifida darduma ta tarar da sallah dama tayi alwala a bayi kan ta fito tana idarwa tayi azkar tana cikinyi mama ta shigo tace harkin dawo tace ehh mama na shigo kina sallah mama tace ehhh naji shigo warki ya makaranta da test dinda kukayi hope kin amsa da kyau eh wlh mama duka na amsa mama tace yauwa habibbatyna Allah yamiki albarka yabaki sa'a a karatun ki tace Ameen first love dina murmushi kawai mama tayi tace inkin gama kisauko tace to mama mama ta juya ta jamata kofar bayan ta kamala ta tashi ta linke darduman da hijab dinta ta ajiye kafun taje ta zauna a dressing mirror ta shafa manta da turare tana gamawa ta dau bail din kayan ta daura a kai ta fita parlor mama tace uwar kwalliya da san kamshi tace mama banti kwalliya ba fahhh mai kawai na shafa da turare mama tace I naga alama mijinki zai sha aiki dariya lailah tayi ta boye fuskan ta wai taji kunyya murmushi kawai mama tayi tace ja'ira kawai kwaso coolers dinnan dazu ban kwaso ba tace to first luv dina mama tace Allah ya shirya min ke tace Ameen ta shige kitchen ta kwaso tana gamawa tazo ta zauna a parlor kusa da mama husna nanan taba TV hankalinta sai da lailah ta dungureta kafun ta zauna tana cewa mayyar kallo Allah yasa dai kina abin kirki a school ba shirme kike ba husna tace mama kinga Aunty lailah kohhh tana cemun mayya kuma wai Allah yasa Ina kokari bani ke zuwa na daya ba husna tafada tana tura baki mama dai batace komai ba lailah tace Allah yasadai bada expo(Answer) kike zuwa first ba me husna zatayi banda kuka haba tuni tafada birgima a kasa tana kuka da ihu tana mama kingan tako juyawa mama tayi tana dariya kasa kasa da dai tata abin bana karewa bane tace yi hakuri autana zo abinki I ko ita aunty lailah tasan baki expo kawai dai Neman sokana ne k dai kyaleta gobe zamu outing banda ita Kasai zo abin ki haba husna najin outing ta taso da tsauri dama mayyar yawo ce tazo ta zauna tasa cinyarta akan kafan mama tana kukan shagwaba Banace kiyi shiru ba ko mu fasa fitan ne da tsauri tace a'a mama nayi mai lailah zatayi inba dariya ba tace kai mamar yawo I nan kike con first ba dai a school bahh husana tace mama kinga aunty ko mama tace lailah ki barmin autana ta juya haka mana haba kin dameta kyaleta gobe insha Allah bazamu da ita ba da tsauri lailah tace yi hakuri mama wlh na bari Allah sai kin ba auta hakuri lailah tayi dariya tace to mama, tace auta auta haba auta kyaukya wan yarinyar mamanta mai zuwa ta daya a school da islamiyya haba hussy na zan siya miki ice cream kinji baby hussyna tana daga mata gira tana murmushi tace to za'a je dake amma gobe zaki kawo mun ko tace ehhh insha Allah tace to na hakura dariya mama da lailah sukayi mama tace auta kwadayi saboda ice cream har kin hakura turo baki kawai tayi irin na shagwababbun nan suna hiran su sukaji jiran sallah insha'i mama tace suje suyi sallah su dawo sukace to mama Washe gari ta kasance Wednesday husna tayi shirin school 7:15 ta bar gida driver ya kaita lailah ko batada lecture tana gida tana barci ba ita ta tashi ba sai 10:00  na safe tana tashi ta shiga wanka bayan ta fito ta yi  shafe shafen ta ta sa riga da skirt na less sky blue tayi daurin ture kaga tsiya dama ta kwance kitso tayi doughnut dashi Masha Allah tayi kyau sossai dama lailah ba dai kyau ba kaman ita tayi kanta fara ce doguwa amma ba sossai bahhh tana da jiki kadan not slim not fat tana da coca cola shape tanada dogon gashi har baya she's so cute, tana gamawa ta fita zuwa parlor ta share parlor tayi mop ta gyra TV stand da center table duk ta gogesu tana gamawa ta shiga kitchen ta wanke kwano nin ta tagani a sink bayan ta gama ta share kitchen tayi moping ta fito ta zo ta kunna burner na turare ko Ina ya dau kamshi ta wuce dakinta ta cire bed sheet din tasa wani ta share dakinta tayi mopping sannan ta yi wanking  toilet bayan tagama tayi dakin mama taje ta share ta samata wani bed sheet ta yi mopping ta wanke mata toilet dama mama bata bari suyi na daddy ita keyi dakanta 12:45 ta gama komai taje dakinta ta tube ta daura towel ta shiga wanka 12:55 ta fito bayan tayi alwala  ta shafa mai ta sa Ina wear ta sa plain gown da hijab ta tarar da sallah tana idarwa ta yi adduo'i ta shafa ta tashi ta zauna a dressing mirror tayi light make up ta fesa turare ta tashi ta bude cabinet dinta ta ciro less dinta maroon color riga da skirt tayi daurin Aisha Buhari tasa jewelries dinta ta fito parlor mama na ganita tace Masha Allah habibaty kinyj kyau sai Ina wannan wankan haka dariya kawai lailah tayi tace ba inda zani mama tazo ta zauna kusa da mama bayan minti 5 mama tace ta duba abincin dake wuta tace to taje ta duba ta samu ya nuna ta saukar ta juye a cooler ta aje gefe sannan ta bude fridge ta dauko pineapple da apple da banana duk tayi juice dinsu ta diba duka tasa a jug ta ajiye ma daddy nashi a fridge nasuma tasa kafun su ci abinci tana gamawa ta dauko abincin ta ajiye a dining ta dawo parlor ta ce na gama mama ta kwanta ta daura kanta saman cinyan mama mama na shafa kanta suna hira. 

2:00 husna ta dawo daga school tatadda su mama a parlor ta rungume mama ta gaishe ta da Anty lailah, lailah tace ni dai kar akaryamin uwa wannan irin rungumar Allah yasa dai baki gautar mata da kashi ba ta karasa da dariya mama na tayata turo biki husna tayi tace mama kinga Aunty lailah koh kullun sai ta nemi tsokana na mama tace kyaleta autana zamu pasa zu outing din da ita da tsauri lailah ta rungume mama tana bata hakuri tace ayya mamana farin cikina first love dina my happiness my world my everything haba mamana ayi hakuri bazan sake ba dariya mama tayi tace I dama k awurin dadin baki A1 tuni sai ki chuci mutun da dadin baki dariya lailah tayi tace ayi hakuri mama aje dani mama tace tor naji uwar zance da dadin baki lailah tace yauwa mamana tana ma husna gwalo husna tace mama kinga aunty lailah ko, mama tace nikam ban iyawa da fitinar ku ta tashi tana tafiya tace  kuyi ku shirya karfe uku (3) zamu tafi sukace to mama duk suka amsa da to mama lailah ta wuce dakinta hakama husna, husna na shiga ta cire uniform dinta ta daura towel ta shiga wanka bayan tafito ta shafa lotion dinta ta fesa turare tayi make up tayi kyau Masha Allah  ta tashi ta bude cabinet dinta ta chiro material mai kyau da kudin shi zai kai kimanin dubu 50 pink color bayan ta ciro tasa kayanta tayi dauri mai kyau na zamani ta dauko gyale pink da takalmi da jaka duk pink bayan ta gama shiri ta kara fesa turare autan mama tayi kyau masha Allah, a ban garen lailah bayan ta shiga daki wanka ta shiga bayan ta fito ta zauna ta shafa lotion dinta bayan ta gama ta hau make up tana kammalawa ta tashi ta bude drawer dinta ta chiro gezna dinta mai kalan sky blue dogon rigane ya sha aiki daga sama har kasa bayan ta ciro ta aje akan gado ta bude bangaran inners ta chiro Wanda zata sa sky blue da dogon Levi's shima sky blue  bayan ta chiro tasa kafin tasa gown dinta tana gamasawa ta kunche gashin ta tashafa mai tayi combing tayi doughnut dashi sannan ta daura dankwali tayi kyau sossai kaman ka sace ta ka gudu tana gamawa ta dauko jaka, takalmi, gyale duk kalan kayanta tana kammalawa ta fito tahadu da husna itama ta fito tace wow antyna kinyi kyau sossai murmushi lailah tayi tace ke ma kinyi kyau Aura tace thanks Aunty lets take selfie ta saya suka dauka kala wurin goma sai da lailah tayi magana kafin husna ta sarara suka sauko parlor after 5min lailah tace husna ta duba mama ko tagama tace to ta haura sama ta bude dakin mama ta sameta azaune tagama shiri tana waya tace mama mun gama girgiza mata kai mama tayi taja mata kofan ta sauko bayan fitan husna da minti goma mama ta sauko tace masha Allah kunyi kyau en matan mama suna tace mama muyi selfie mama ta  tsaya lailah ta dauke su hoto, mama tace to muje tunda angama sukace to lailah ta kashe TV mama da husna suka fita bayan lailah             TUSHEN LABARI 

Alhaji Ibrahim yakasance uba ga Alhaji Harun da Alhaji Muhammad, Alhaji Harun ne babba sai Alhaji Muhammad sukadai Allah ya basu,  Matar shi daya mai suna hajiya khadija suna zaune a garin kastina Amma en asalin gombe ne aiki ne ya kawo su kastina, suna da rufen Asirin su masha Allah, Alhaji Harun da Alhaji Muhammad sunyi karatu tun daga primary zuwa secondary Alhaji harun nada 22 yagama while Alhaji Muhammad nada 21 sakanin su shekara dayane,  bayan kammala seconday school dinsu da shekara daya Allah yayiwa iyayen su rasuwa a hanyyan su na dawowa daga Gombe a sakamakon hatsarin da suka yi, bayan rasuwar iyayen su da shekara daya aka raba musu gado, Akwai fahimta da jituwa sossai a tsakanin su, bayan an raba gado suka fara business,suna samu sossai Dan Allah yasa musu Albarka suna sana'an sai da atanfa sunada babban shago sossai guda uku,  bayan shekara daya Alhaji Harun yakawo shawaran su koma makaranta, Alhaji Muhammad ya amince suka koma karatu Alhaji Harun yasamu Admission a fudma nan kastina university while Alhaji Muhammad ya samu a kasu, Alhaji Harun neh yacigaba da kula da shagunan su in yanada lectures boy's dinsu zasu kula da shagon kafun ya dawo inko babu yana shago, Alhaji Muhammad kuwa sai anyi hutu ko sun Dan samu break yake komawa gida sucigaba da kula da kasuwan cinsu, bayan samun Admission dinsu da shekara uku wato suna three hundred level "300" Alhaji Harun ya hadu da wata budurwa tazo shagon shi, ya yana da hankali, da natsuwan yarinyar, yanemi gidan su yarinyar yaje yanemi izini a gurin babban ta, kuma ya amince Dan ba'a tsayar mata da kowa ba, ahankali soyayya mai karfi ya kullu a tsakanin su

Alhaji Muhammad kuma ya hadu da tashi budurwar a makaranta department dinsu ba daya ba,  daga Dan  gaidawa irin na mutunci,  Dan yarinyar badai natsuwa ba ga tarbiya shi,  Ana cikin haka ya bayyana mata da soyayyar shi dama itama tana sanshi saboda yana da natsuwa ga Addini,ta amince suka fara soyayya mai tsabta Dan an san da zanchan a gidan su kuma an bashi izini amma ance sai ta mammal karatu za'ayi mata aure yace ba damuwa ya amince

Bayan kammala karatun su da shekara biyu da gama kara tunsu Alhaji Harun yayi aure, bayan surenshi da wata shida Alhaji Muhammad ma yayi aure ankashe kudi abikin kaman ba'asan ciwan su ba, duk sunyi ginin su a kastina kowa da gidan shi, bayan shekara daya da auren Alhaji Harun matarshi ta haihu namiji anci an sha anyi rabo masha Allah, yaro yaci sunnan baban su Ibrahim Ana kiran shi da Abba,  sai dai muce Allah yaraya mana Ibrahim"Abba" bayan shekara goma kuma ta haifi twins 👬 duka mata zokuga Murna gun family Tara sukaci suna Raihan"Hassana da Ramlat"Hussaina" bayan shekara uku ta haifi namiji yaci sunnan Muhammad Auwal, shekara Ibrahim "Abba" 28 twins nada 18 auta nada 15,Abba yagama university 🎓 har yafara aiki a company su baban shi shine MD, while twins na university 200 level  auta na Sss3

Alhaji Muhammad basu samu haihuwa da wuri ba sai bayan shekara nine "9" kafun mama ta samu ciki zo kuga murna gun family sossai daddy ke tattalin mama kaman kwai mosi kadan zaice mai kike so meke damun ki, wata Tara chip mama ta santalo yar baby ta kyaukya wa ranar suna baby taci sunnan maman daddy khadija Ana kiran ta da lailah anyi rabo a sunnan sossai mai jego da baby sun samu kyauta sossai daddy akwati uku yayima baby, maman baby ko set yayi mata Alhaji Harun Wanda ake Kira da baba set yayima baby  maman baby ta samu akwati two "2" banda Wanda suka samu wurin abokan Arziki, bayan suna suka cigaba da kula da babyn su,  bayan shekara biyar mama ta kara samun ciki daddy yayi murna sossai har sadaka yayi nagode ma Allah daya kara bashi haihuwa har yacire rai da kara samun baby, bayan wata tara ta haihu baby girl ran suna baby taci sunan Asma'ul Husna amma Ana kiranta da Husna daga kanta haihuwa ya tsaya mata, lailah na university yanzu tana karantar nursing tana level 300 inda Husna ke sss2,lailah nada shekara 19 husna nada 14.

Alhaji muhammad Aliyu shahararen mai kudine a nigeria gabadaya yanada mata daya da yara hudu mata biyu maza biyu, Aisha, Izzatu, Zaid sai auta sa'ad, sunan mahaifiyar su hajiya Fatima Muhammad kabir tana da shekara 70 kyaukyawan dattijuwa ce kaman balarabiya, Aisha nada shekara 36 tanada yara biyar, Afra, Ashna, Fauwaz, Suhail, Jawad tana aure a Abuja tana auren wani babban dan kasuwa, izzatu nada shekara 34 tanada Tara 4 Amra, Hydar, suhan, Ammar tana auren wani dan siyasa tana zaune a kaduna da mijinta, Zaid nada shekara 32 ya karanci doctor 💉  har ya gama karatun shi yanzu haka Akwai Asibitin da daddyn shi ya gina mai suna AIZS SPECIALIST HOSPITAL shi doctor 💉  ne ban garan zuciya, zaid nada aure ya hadu da matar shi a wani course da yaje yi a makkah last  three "3" years, sunnan ta Airah, Airah nada shekara 23 shekaran su uku da aure yaran su biyu Muhammad ana kiranshi da Ameen sai Hauwa'u ana jiranta da jiddah, sai auta sa'ad ya karenci doctor 💉 bangaren mata yanzu haka yana lecturing kuma yana zuwa asibitin su, sa'ad hadadan guy ne son kowa kin Wanda yarasa dogo ne fari yana da saje da kwantatcen gashi baki  sidik  ya sha gyra zan ban sha'awa kaman balarabe, yanada dan jiki kadan his not slim nor fat nrlm dan daidai yana da shekara 28,sa'ad miskiline na bugawa a jarida baya magana sai ya kama, inkaga yayi magana mai dan tsayi to yana tare da family shi mummy kance nikam zanga matar da zata aureka da wannan halin, sa'ad nada hankalin ga natsuwa, jwarjini, cikan haiba, respect, hakuri, sadaka, tausayi, bazaka gane hakan ba sai ka zauna dashi wasu na mishi kallon mai girman kai ko mai takama saboda rashin hayaniyan shi da son magana.

Sa'ad bait zarce ko Ina ba sai masallacin dake layin su, bashi ya fito ba sai bayan anyi isha'i ya fito a masallacin, yazo zai shiga mota sai ya yaga kaman motan Hydar yagani, ya duba da kyau ya ga shine dai, jingina yayi da jikin motan shi ya nade hannun shi a kirjin shi yana kallon hanyyan da ake fitowa daga masallacin bayan kaman minti 3 sai gashi ya fito hada ido suka yi da Hydar din, direct zuwa mai parlor, shiga sukayi da bismillah babu kowa a ciki dan salame ta tafi part dinta yin sallah, zama Hydar yayi akan kujera mai cin mutun biyu, aje jakan hannun  shi yayi a kan kujera ya nufi suka tsauko parlor da mummy Direct kitchen mummy tayi, sa'ad na ganin ta nufi kitchen ya tashi ya bita, shi ya fito da tiran da mummy tajero musu abinci akai ajewa yayi a katsa saman carpet, dukawa Karfe 5:00 dai-dai su ka tashi daga makaranta, jerawa sukayi da en akin su su hudu suna tafiya har suka fita daga gate din makarantar, suna fita sai ga driver biyun yazo, daya daga cikin su mai suna Fatima amman ana kiranta da zahra tace muzo muje mana, sai mu aje ku, shiru lailah tayi tana kallan su "alkawarin ta da sir Sa'ad ne ya fado mata a rai" k khady mai kike tunani zo muje mana, a'a kuyi hakuri kuje, daddy yace naje na same shi, ta karashe da istigfari a zuciyar ta,okh!  Toh!  Bye- Bye sai gobe, daga mutsu hannu tayi murmushi dauke a fuskan ta har suka bace ma ganin ta, tafiya ta fara zuwa bakin titi, horn kawai taji a bayan ta, har ta dan razana, ta riga da tasan waye ne, tsayawa tayi ya karaso yayi parking ya bude mata kofan mai zaman banza, takowa take a hankali har takai, shiga tayi ta zauna sannan ta gaishe shi, "amsawa yayi yana kallon ta" tana ganin sun hada ido tayi tsaurin dauke idonta,"lfy lau bby ykk ya gida da mamana da kowa" amsa mishi tayi kan ta a duke tana wasa da yasan hannun ta. "Ya alkawari na, hope an shirya min amsa mai kyau" shiru tayi bata amsa mai ba sai jan hijabin ta take, "murmushi yayi yana tsosa Kenya, shi dai mace mai kunyya na birge shi abin da yasa yake karan san ta kenan, yau dai duk kunyan ki sai kin ban amsa na bazan yadda ba" yanda yayi maganan ne yaso bata dariya amman ta gimse, "tada motan yayi ya cillan hancin motan kan titi, tuki yake a hankali, yanayi yana dan yi mata hira, "sama sama take amsa mai" parking yayi a layin su amman a kwai gida biyu kafin nasu lailah, juyowa yayi yana kallan ta yace" baby today is monday cika min alkawari na, shiru tayi bata amasa mai ba, yace beauty talk nw pls yana marairai ce fuska, ya bayin da taji muryan shi ne yasa ta dagowa ta kalle shi, tsaukar da kwayan idan ta tayi akan fuskan shi taga yanda ya wani shagwabe fuskan kaman dan yaro , murmushi tayi har hakoran ta suka bayyana kafin ta dauke kai still da murmushi a fuskan ta, "bby pls nw ko sai na miki kuka ne" girgiza mishi kai tayi alaman A'a, "tohhh!  Tell me pls" ita kam mamaki yake bata jibi kaman ba shi ne mai murtuke fuskan ba, da miskilancin tsiya. Tsawan daya daka mata ne ya hanata karasawa yace leave I said kuma koda wasa kar in kara ganin ki a office dina, wawiya mara tarbiya kawai, yana aika ma da wani banzan kallo,hannu rasa tana share hawayen da suka wanke mata fuska, sir dan Allah ka tausaya min ba yin kaina bane, banta ba cewa inasan wani ba Allah ne ya jara beni ta karasa tana tsaka gwiwowin ta a katsa ta kara fashewa da matsanancin cin kuka, ya tsani jin kukan mace baya so, toh kiyi shiru mai kike so na miki yanzu, a gaskiya zance bazan iya ba sai dai na baki hakuri, Allah ya baki Wanda ya fini, kallan shi take hawaye na tsiyaya a idan ta, yace kuyi hakuri kinji Allah ya baki wani Wanda ya fini, ina da wacce nakeso, sir zan so wacce kake so nayi Alkawari zan zama ta biyu in ka amin ce zan maka biyayya insha Allah, " ido kawai ya zuba mata shikam yaga abinda yafi karfin shi, ki dai you hakuri, amman taki sai maimai ta magana daya take, dayaga alaman dai ba yarda zatayi ba sai yace kije zan yi shawara.  Tashi tayi tana share hawayen idan ta tace nagode, Allah tsaka da Alkairi, hakika in ka amshi bukatana ka cece ni, ka taimaki iyaye na, nagode sossai, Allah ya biyaka ya tsareka aduk inda kake, yaji dadin Addu'an da tayi mai har yadan yi murmushi, amman Maganan da tati kafin sannan ne ya daure mai kai bai gane kan zanchan ba. Sallama ta mai ta fita a office din. Bayan ta fita ya jingina da kujera yana tunanin alamarin daya faru yanzu, ya shafe minti 30 yana tunani kafin ya cigaba da aikin shi, karfe 5:00 dai-dai ya fara had a kayan shi fitowa yayi rataye da jakan laptop din shirt, parking space ya nufa ya shiga motan shi, daukan waya yayi, yayi dailing number ringing uku aka dauka, in kun gama lectures ki fito ina parking space, "zaro ido tayu tace wani parking space", na cikin makaranta inda nake parking mana, nikam bazan iya ba, lokacin fahhh! Duk yawanci an tashi, sai aga na shiga motan ka, tabdi za'a sami news din da za'ayi gobe kenan hmun, "toh!  Ina zan jira ki" inda ka saba dauka na, bye ina lectures sai na fito ta katse kiran. Bayan sun gama lectures sun fito sai ga driver kawarta, hamdala tati dayaga yazo da wuri, bayan ta tafi sai ta nufi waken gate din, hago motan shi tayi a pake, wurin motan ta nufa, knocking glass din motan tayi, hannu yasa ya bude mata yace I a bude yake, shiga tati suka wuce. Haka Rayuwa ta cigaba da tafiya sai shan love din su suke ba kama hannun yaro, lailah din da bata fita amman yanzu ta iya neman excuse din fita in zasu je wurin shakatawa da babyn ta.

yayi ya shiga, Ammi da Hydar yagani a parlor, dukawa yayi ya gaishe da Ammi yace ya bakon wata,  amsawa tayi da kulawa tace bakon wata Alhmd ya Ka bar mummy da daddy, lafiyan su lau sun cema na gaishe ki, ina amsawa, hora sukan Danyi bayan kaman minti 30 sai suka fita zuwa dakin Hydar, hira sukayi irrin nasu na samari kafin Sa'ad ya fara bashi labarin little jerry shi, Hydar ne tace taushe zamuje gidan su, shiru Sa'ad yayi sannan yace a gidan su ba'a barin ta tadi, sai ta gama karatu and tana last year ne yanzu, sa tom Hydar ya amsa sannan ya tashi suka fita, parlor mummy suka nufa yayi mata sallama, Ammi tace bazakayi bude baki anan ba, "A'a Ammi amman zanzo wata rana insha Allah" sa toh!  Ammi ta Amsa sannan tace a gaida gida, ka gaishe da mummy tana mika mai wani laida, amsa yayi yai mata godiya sannan suka fita...........................

Washe gari tun karfe 5:00 duk suka ta shi,Kuma ba su koma ba,batan sun Yi sallah aikin gida suka kamayi,karfe 9:00 dai-dai suka Gama komi da komi,wanka duk sukayi,mummy ne ta kawo musu Kaya sabbi Kal a laida,atanfa ne Mai kyau Mai tsada,amsa Lailah tayi tayi mata godia sossai,bayan sunyi wanka Suka shirya cikin kayan da mummy ta Bata,sukayi kwalliya sossai,dai-dai sun gama makeup suna hoto mummy ta shigo da laida a hannun ta tace,daughter gashi,da tsauri Lailah ta karaso ta amshi laidan tayi ma mum godiya sossai, bayan mum ta fita ta bude laida ta ga takalmi ne da gyle sai jaka,masu mugun kyau,Raihan ne tace Lailah mu gani,nuna mats jakan tayi,da wow Raihan ta Masa tace kaiiiiiii! Haka dai rayuwa ta cigaba da tafiya inda ayau su Lailah zasu Fara exam din su na first semester 400level.Tun karfe 4:00 ta tashi Dan paper safe gare ta,bayan tagama duk abinda zatayi,wurin karfe bakwai ta bar gida saboda tanasan ta danyi karatu kafin a shiga paper,ba ita ta Kai ba sai karfe 7:20,direct bushiyan da ke kusa da lecture hall din su ta zauna tana ta karatun ta,8 dai-dai ko da minti daya ba'a karaba aka Fara paper,dan dama angaya ma student duk Wanda yayi latti shiyasani,sai wurin goma Suka fito paper sai murmushi wasu student suke daga harda Lailah a cikin su,wasu ko ko alamun murmushi babu,da musalin 1 Suka shiga Dan biyu zasuyi,karfe 4 suka fito,batan ta fito ta nufi office din Sa'ad ta Mai gidiyan hidiman da yayi musu sannan ta fita,haka dai suka cigaba da rubuta paper inda Alhamdullah babu Wanda Yaba ma Lailah wahala sossai,ranar Friday inda aranar be zasu Gama paper,wurin 12 take makaranta Dan 1 suke da paper,ko da ta Isa 12:30 saboda goslow da k hanyya,da zuwan ta baifi minti 20 ba aka shiga paper Suka fito karfe 4,inda aka Fara murnan angama paper tsaura aga results,inda kowa sai Addu'an yaga Mai kyau kawai yake,itako Lailah tana tare da kawar ta sai hiran su suke,dama already ta Gaya ma driver time,Yana zuwa ko ya dauke ta Suka nufi gida,tun ranar karshe Bata sake zuwa school ba sai bayan sati 2 inda a ranar ne zasu Fara second semester 4:00 level,haka dai rayuwa ta cigaba da tafiya inda ayanzu sunci Rabin semester,ayanzu me Sa'ad yake damun ta akan tanasan ya gabatar da kanshi a gidan su wato ya turo Neman izini,sai tace mishi yayi hakuri ba wai batasan ya turo bane a'a daddy ne yace sai tagama karatu kafin Nan zata Fara maganan zance shiko da tace mishi haka sai ya kyale ta kawai,haka rayuwa ta cigaba da tafiya inda haryanxu suna kan waya da Abba Shima sama sama Dan bawani ba shi lokaci take ba,ace war ta wai Bata San shi,ga Wanda take so Kuma shi zata Aura,"Niko nace zamu gani iiiiiiiii!". Yanzu haka project suke a makaranta inda ranar Monday zasu Fara presenting,ita Lailah ba'a samu matsala a nata ba,da yake Sa'ad me yayi mata komi har mamaki lecturers suke ya akayi komi nata yayi,sati daya sukayi suna presenting kafin aka gama,bayan sati biyu dagamawan su aka Fara jarabawa,sun dau sati biyu suna jarabawa kafin Suka gama, lfy Alhmd,sun kammala karatun su lafiya tsaura a ga results Biki ya karato inda yau ake Fulani day kowa ka gani a wurin da kayan Fulani yake/take,wasu fari,wasu kuma Baki,wasu ko blue black,biki yayi Baki inda kannen ango da kawayen Amarya k ta tashewa da wasu samari a cikin filin rawa,Amarya ko Tasha kayan Fulani inda kayan ta ya fita daban Dana kowa,Ango ma haka,kayan shi ya fita daban Dana tsauran abokan shi,basu suka gama event din ha sai gab da magariba,inda kowa ya watse Amman wasu ko na nan suna ta rawa,Amarya da Ango da wasu kawayen ta da kannen ta ko sunyi gaba,inda kowa ya koma a gajiye Amman Banda Lailah Dan ita bawani rawan kirki tayi ba Dan ita dama ba iya rawan tayi ba,Wanka suka shiga 1 by 1. sai hawaye take a zaune,hannu tasa ta dauke hawayen ta gyara idan,Amman duk da haka sai an gane tayi kuka,bayan sun Gama bada labari,sai aka ba ma Ango  umurnin a bude fuskan Amaryar shi,bude fuskan ta yayi in da wurin ya dau tafi raf raf raf,tun da aka bude fuskan ta kan ta k kasa,kawayen Amarya aka Kira da kannen Amarya da en uwa Amman en Mata,fitowa duk sukayi inda aka sake mutsu gida,suka Fara taka rawa cikin natsuwa da class, twins da Suka Sha dinki iri daya har da Husna,Suka Ware kansu wuri daya suna rawa da class,MC ne yayi magana inda Suka nufi wuraren zaman su,wayan Raihan ne ya dau ringing inda ta nufi hanyyan fita a hall din,wurin motan amare ta nufa inda ta jin Gina da jikin motan tana waya,batan ta Gama ta juya zata wuce sai kawai taga mutun a kanta ta mugun tsorata,da tsauri guy din yace Mata calm down pls am not here to hurt you,shiru kawai Raihan tayi tana kallan shi,inda Hydar yace in bazaki damu ba inasan muyi magana ko Zaki bani number ki,Dan shiru Raihan tayi na minti 5 sannan tace ban San ka ba tana turo Baki,Dan murmushi guy din yayi sannan yace Zaki sanni Amman with time, atakai ce dai sunana Aliyu,but anfi kirana da Hydar,shiru Raihan tayi Bata ce komai ba,Mai sunan ki pls sister,dago kanta tayi ta kalle shi sai tayi tsaurin tsauke idan ta kasa,bude bakin ta tayi Ahankali tace"My name is Raihanat" Dan lumshe ido yayi yace nyc name,pls give me ur contact pls,Mika mishi hannu tayi alamun ya Bata wayan,Mika Mata wayan yayi,amsa tayi ta saka mishi number sannan ta Mika mai,karba yayi yace thank u Angel,dagowa tayi ta kalle shi suka hada Ido minti 5 Shima ya shigo. Wurin zaman su ya nufa inda ya tadda Sa'ad Yana ta operating wayan shi,MC ne ya Kira Amarya akan su fito suyi rawa,Abba ne ya Fara Mike was sannan ya rike hannun Lailah ya dagota inda ya mikar da ita tashi tayi ta Fara binshi a baya har suka nufi tsakiya filin,juyata ya Fara Yi a hankali ita ko komi batayi Yan da ya bi da ita haka take bi,en uwa da abokan arziki me suka fito Suka Fara yi musu liki,ta shi Sa'ad Shima yayi inda ya shiga cikin filin yako ci sa'a ya Kai har inda Amaryar take,magana yayi mata,Babu Wanda ya ji bayan ita da shi Dan koni bansan Mai aka ce ba,Yana Gama Mata magana ya fita a filin,daga Kai tayi ta bi bayan shi da kallo har ya fita a cikin hall din,Yana fita ta tsauke kanta kasa tare da tsauke net din kasa,kuka ta farayi amman ba Mai Kara ba,tun da ya tafi ko juyawan ma Bata Kara Yi ba,bayan sun koma wurin zaman su ta cigaba da kukan ta har aka tashi,anci an sha a bikin komai ya tafi Yan da akeso Kuma aka tsara,wurin karfe 11:30pm aka tashi,Amman kafin a Gama kwasan mutane sai 12:14am.




Yau Thursday inda za'ayi kamu,wurin karfe 2:00pm aka Fara shirye shiryen biki,a cikin tsakar gida za'ayi cikin gidan ya tsaru an gyara komai yayi kyau,karfe 4:00 pm aka Fara gudanar da Event,inda komai ya tafi dai-dai yayi kyau ya kawatu,an raba kasko,da lalle da da gishirin lalle sai jakan abinci da snacks,wurin sallan magariba aka gama inda kowa ya watse.



Yau Friday inda yau ne ranar daurin Aure,bayan An tsauko sallah  juma'a aka daura Auren "IBRAHIM  HARUN (ABBA) & NANA KHADIJA MUHAMMAD (LAILAH) akan tsadaki naira dubu dari biyu lakadan ba ajalan ba,bayan an daura Aure,mutanen cikin gida suka Fara guda, Lailah najin gudan mutane a cikin gida ta Fara hawaye shikenan an daura ta shi tayi ta shiga bayi inda ta zauna taci kuka har ta gode Allah,tana zaune a bayi tana kukan ta aka shigo ne manta Dan a shirya ta,tashi tayi da tsauri ta cire kayan jikin ta ta hada ruwan wanka,bayan tayi wanka ta fito daure da towel daya Kai gwiwa,tana shiga daki akasa ta zauna kan kujera,kwalliya za'a Fara Mata Amman ta hana, lotion dinta ta dauka ta shafa bayan ta Gama aka yi mata kwalliyan,less Mai kalan pink da adon stone a jikin shi tasa, pictures aka Fara yi,ana cikin hoto sai Suka ji guda daga waje,ana Ango Ango kasha kamshi,gyra Mata gyale(vein) dinta akayi aka fito da ita waje,hotuna aka Fara yi,bayan an Gama hoto Ango da abokan shi Suka tafi,tun da Lailah ta shiga cikin daki ta kwanta a kan gado saboda wani irin ciwon kan da ta keji a sakamakon kukan da taci dazu,dungulewa tayi akan gado,mummy ne ta shigo da wasu kayan da Lailah zata sa akalla kala goma,samun Lailah tayi a kan gado ta dungule,tsaurin karasawa wurin Lailah tayi tace, daughter na Mai ya faru,ta fada tana Kai hannun ta jikin Lailah,wani irin zafi taji ya hugi hannun ta,tun kafin ta tabata ma tajiyo hucin zafin jikin ta,Kiran sunan ta ta farayi,da kyar Lailah ta bude idan ta ta Dan kalle ta,a rikice mummy ta Fara tan bayana ta Mai ya same ta,taba kanta Lailah tayi alamun shi ke  Mata ciwo, tashi mummy tayi da tsauri ta fita a dakin,tsabar da mama tayi sannan ta Kira family doctor din su,dakin suka koma da mama,batan kaman minti 15 doctor yazo inda aka kawo shi har dakin Lailah, dubata ya Fara Yi,bayan ya gama ya saka Mata ruwan drip tare dayin wasu allurori a ciki,Yana gamawa ya rubuta ma mummy maganin da za'a siya Mata,mama ne tace Mai k damun ta,bayani doctor yayi mutsu,inda yace gaskiya tasa makanta damuwa,Amman Alhmd da tsauki baiyi worse ba,sai dai a Yi kokarin gani ta cire da kuwan saboda gudun matsala,Yana gama yimutsu bayani ya fita a dakin,Zama mama tayi a kusa da Lailah,tana tausaya ma Yar ta ita kadai tasan batasan Auren Dan ta gargade ta akan Kar ta kuskura ta bari kowa sani,suna zaune su duka acikin daki su Twins da Husna sai wasu daga cikin kawayen Lailah harda Zainab a cikin su,samun Lailah sukayi da drip a hannu,da tsauri duk Suka karasa suna tanbayan Mai ya same ta,mama ne tace zazzabi ke damun ta, tausayayin tane duk ya kamasu.wurin karfe  4 drip din ya kare,idan ta biyu ya kare,batan ya kare ta zare syringe din daga jikin ta,mikewa tayi bakin ta da Bismillah bayi ta shiga ta Yi wanka da Alwala bayan ta fito ta shafa lotion sannan ta saka dogon hijabi,darduma ta shinfida tayi sallah bayan ta idar tayi Addu'a ta tashi sai Husna ta hau,Zama  tayi aka Fara Mata make up, bayan angama Mata make up aka Bata wani less da kudin shi ya Kai kimanin dubu 75,bayan tasaka kayan ka Mata dauri,sannan aka saka Mata Alkebba kalan kayan,ta kalmin ta ma Mai cover ne sai walkiya da haske yake,jakan ta ma haka,komai sai da yake da shigen kalan kayan jikin ta,bayan an gama shirya ta wuraren karfe 8:00pm,dakin iyayen Mata aka kaita first bayan sungana Mata huduban su aka kaita na iyayen ta Maza Suma Suka Yi,bayan sun gama aka Kara maida it's wurin mama in da anan me akaga ruwan kuka duk kuka suke mama tun tana dannewa har takasa,fuskan ta ta kulle da gyalen ta tana ta share hawayen,Husna ki makalkale Lailah tayi tana ta sharban kuka dama gata da tsaurin kuka,lalashin su en uwa da Abokan arziki suka Fara yi,da kyar aka babbare Husna daga jikin lailah,dauka ta akayi za'a fita da ita,kuka ta saki da karfi ta juyo da gudu ta fada kan mama ta saki kuka Mai tsauti kafin ta Fara magana,tace mama kimin Addu'a Allah ya bani ikon Zama da miji na lfy,da Yi mishi biyayya,ya bani ikon son shi da danne zuciya murguda Baki, murmushi kawai Abba yake Dan duk abin da take Bai bashi haushi Asali ma Kara kyau take mishi da sa shi nishadi.bayan ta fito daga bandaki ta nufi inda ya shinfida darduma ta tsaya,juyawa yayi ya tayar da sallah,bayan sun idar ya Kama kanta tayi Mata Addu'oi sossai sannan ya Fara Mata tambayoyi,kumbure kumbure take tana bashi amsa sai wani hura hanci take,shiko Yana same da ita Amman yayi Mata banza,ta shi yayi ya dauko Kazan dake saman fridge din dake dakin ta ya ajiye a gaban ta,kallan ta yayi sannan yace je ki dauko Mana plate,dagowa tayi ta kalle shi,har zatayi mutsu ko Mai ta tuna oho,mikewa tayi ta fita a dakin,bayan ta fita daga dakin tsauka kasa tayi ta Fara bire duk kofan da ta gani Dan gaskiya Bata San wanne ne kitchen ba,ta bude kofa uku a cikin kofofi 6 data gani a down stairs,tsayawa tayi tasa hannu a kwankwaso,tana kallon duka kofofin,ji kawai tayi an taba ta ta baya,razana tayi zatayi ihu yayi tsaurin rikota ya rungume ta,rike shi tayi sossai Dan ta tsorata,tsabida Bata ji zuwan shi ba,bayan kaman minti 5 ya cire ta daga jikin shi,hannun ta ya Kama ya jata zuwa kitchen din,shiga sukayi a tare Suka dau abin bukata sannan Suka koma daki,ajiye try da plate din sukayi,Abba ne yayi serving din su Dan tasan Lailah bazata yi ba,bayan yasa ya ce ta ci,kin ci tayi,shiru yayi Yana kallon ta,sai da ya mai-mai ta Mata tsau uku kafin tasa hannu ta Fara Dan tsakura,ko Rabin mama Bata ciba ta cire hannu,kallan ta Abba yayi yace ya Kika cire hannu,"na koshi"kallan ta yayi yace Mai Kika ci,"girgiza mishi Kai tayi alamun Babu komi,tashi yayi ya dawo kusa da ita,Yana Zama kusa da ita gaban ta ya Fara bugun 9-9 hannu yasa ya rike ta ya kwantar da kanta kan kadadan shi sannan ya Fara Bata Naman a baki,kamshi tularan shi da ta Shaka ne yayi Mata Dadi har ta Fara amsan Kazan Bata sani ba,haka ya dinga Bata har taci sossai,5Alive ya Bata tasha sannan ya Bata ruwa bayan ta Sha ya dau tissue ya share Mata bakin ta,jinginar da ita yayi da jikin shi sannan ya Fara ci shima,Bai wani ci dayawa ba ya kulle kazan,cire kanta yayi a jikin shoulder din shi sannan ya tashi ya saka tsauran Kazan a fridge,kafin ya Kai kwanon da aka Bata kitchen ya dauriye su sannan ya dawo dakin,ko days dawo samun ta yayi a gado ta lullube da bargo,shiga yayi ya kashe wutan dakin dama already yayi Shirin barcin shi.takawa ya farayi a hankali har ya karaso bakin gadon,"Lailah ko tun daya bude kofan kirjin ta ya Fara luguden tara tara har zuwa zaman shi a gefen gado,ko motsi batayi ba Dan Kar ya gane batayi barci ba".Bayan ya Gama abin da zai yi ya kwanta akan gado sannan ya ja bargo,bayan kaman minti 30 barci ya kasa daukan shi haka dai yayi ta tunani daga baya dai ya Dan matsa kusa da ita kadan gap din dake tsakanin su bai da yawa,tunanin yanda zai to yake chan dai yayi jarumtar juyo ta sannan ya rungume,"dama Lailah barci bawai nauyi yayi ba,Dan a tsorace take".tana jin ya juya ta ta bude Baki zata sandara ihu yayi tsaurin yaushe mata Baki da nashi,tuni wutar nepan ta ya dauke,mukus tayi jikin ta sai kyarma yake,sai da ya shafe minti 15 Yana Abu daya kafin ya tsake ta,tapin bayan ta yake Yana Mata gagana a kunne,Ni dai banji Mai akace ba,gani kawai nayi ta natsu,haka dai ya dinga Mata har barci ya dauke su,washe gari Abba ne ya Fara tashi bayan ya Yi Alwala ya dawo ya tashe ta sannan ya fita masallaci,ko daya dawo masallaci samun ta yayi kwance a kan  darduma hakan me ya tabbatar mishi da tayi sallah Dan Bai tsaya har ta tashi ba tsabida ya kusa makara a masallaci,Yana zuwa ya zunkuce sai kan gado,tana jin an dauke ta ta firgita,kwantar da ita yayi a jikin shi sannan yace k nine, kina da shegen tsoro dayawa,komai Bata ce mishi ba,kwantar da ita yayi akan gado,Aiko tana kwantawa ta gyara kwanciya ta ja bargo ta rufe jikin ta sannan ta cigaba da barcin ta,ba ita ta tashi ba sai karfe 9 da minti 20,ko da ta tashi bayi ta shiga tayi wanka da brush,fitowa tayi daga ita sai towel dai dai Rabin cinya,ko Rabin ma Bai ida kaiwa ba,dude kofan tayi ta nufi gurin dressing mirror ta Fara shafa Mai,tazo zata shafa a jiki sai tasa hannu zata kwance towel sai jikin ta ya Bata kaman akwai Wanda ke kallon ta,Aiko tana juyawa taga Abba a tsaye kusa da kofa,haba tana ganin shi tayi tsaurin durkusawa,shiko murmushi yayi sannan ya Fara takawa a hankali har ya karaso inda take,ya na zuwa yasa hannu zai daga ta,dunkule jikin ta tayi Taki tsaki,yunkurin dagata Kuma yayi Aiko ta saki kuka tana rokon shi daya kyale ta,Yana ganin tana kuka ya tsaki Baki Yana kallon ta,sannan yace Mai na Miki,girgiza mishi kai tayi,kallan ta yake sannan yace na fita kike nufi,daga mishi Kai tayi da tsauri, murmushi yayi ya shafi gemun shi tare da tsosa kan shi ya fita a dakin,raka shi tayi da harara bayan ya fita tace Dan rainin wayyo mutun zai shigan ma daki ba sallama tsaki ta ja sannan ta mike ta Isa shafa manta kafin ta Nemo Kaya.


Haka dai rayuwa ta cigaba da tafiya inda a yanzu sun kwashe wata 2 Amman haryan zu ba abin da ya shiga tsakanin su,sai dai akwai Dan fahimta akan da.

A bangaren Sa'ad ko Allhah yasa soyayya Lailah ya dan yi tsauki a zuciyar shi dan har give Yana tuna ta,gidan su ko an matsa Mai ya kawo Mata inda shi bai da ma wacce zai gabatar a matsayin wacce zai Aura.

Yau Wednesday inda Sa'ad k da lecture karfe 2 na rana,tun karfe 1:30 yake makaranta inda 2 dai-dai ya shiga aji Aiko Yana shiga en class din ba sufi su 40 ba out of 110 student,Aiko Yana Gama lecture ya ce a tanka paper za'ayi test,da tanka paper da koman su duk a cikin minti biyar a kayi,in ka rubuta oho in ma bakayi ba oho,iko sai da yabi duka desk din ya kwashe papers din Bayan ya sa duk sun daga hannu sama, Mika ma Aisha yayi sannan yace ta bishi da papers din,amsa tayi ba Dan ranta yaso ba ta bishi zuwa office,yana ta fiya tana binshi a baya da yake Bata da tsauri har ya Kai Bata Kai ba,sai Bayan minti 3 kafun ta karaso,sallama tayi a bakin kofan a kalla yayi tsau biyar kafin ya Bata izinin shiga,bayan ta shiga ta Kara gaishe shi sannanta ajiye mishi papers din zata koma,kiran ta yayi yace tazo ta zauna,bayan ta zauna ya yace kwanakin baya kin gabatar da soyayar ki a gate Ni haka ne ki ba haka bane,daga mishi Kai tayi alamun haka ne duk kunyya ya kamata,toh yanzu in bazaki damu ba zan so ki ban dama na nemi izini a gidan ku,dagowa tayi ta kalle shi da tsauri,suna hada Ido kobta tsaukar da Bata kasa,hawaye be ya taru a idan ta Amman Bata Bari ya fito ba,"kina jina kuwa",girgiza mishi Kai tayi alaman ehhhh!.Toh ki ban Address Yana miko Mata paper,amsa tayi kanta a kasa ta rubuta sannan ta Mika mishi,amsa yayi ya duba sannan ya kalle ta yace zan iya zuwa yau,Abban ki na gida,shiru tayi Bata ce komai ba,"tanbayan ki fa nake ya fada ranshi a Dan hade Amman Bai Bari ya sani ba"daga mishi Kai tayi Alamun ehhhhh! Sannan ta tashi zata fita a dakin,Kiran ta yayi,tsayawa tayi a inda take taba jiran ya Fadi abin da zai ce,Baki bani number ba,Kuma bansan abin ya dau lokaci inasan Nan da 1 month ayi komai in ba damuwa,tsaurin juyowa tayi ta kalle shi Dan Jin abin da yace,suna hada Ido ta tsaukar da Bata Dan bazata iya jure kallan idan shi ba,tafiya take kaman kwai ya fashe Mata a ciki,daukan biro tayi ta rubuta number sannan ta ajiye ta juya tana Allah Allah Kar ya kirata,aiko ta cu sa'a Bai kirata ba har ta fita,tana kulle office din ta jingina da kofan tana tsauke ajiyar zuciya,hawaye ne k tsauka daga idan ta sai Jan majina take,ta shafe minti 3 a tsaye kafin tasa kasan nayafin ta ta share hawayen ta ta gyara fuskan ta sannan ta bar wurin.




Bayan Aisha ta Kai gida sai zukkumi da tunani take,Allah yasa dadyn ta ya yarda Dan yace Bai Mata Sure har sai ta kammala karatun ta,tun da ta dawo ta kasa cin abinci,mummy tayi tanbayar duniyan Nan Amman amsan dayane Babu komi,haha dai mummy ta zuba Mata Ido.


Karfe 9 dai-dai yayi ma Sa'ad a kofan gidan su Aisha,horn yayi Mai gadi ya leko yana dubawa ganin Bai San shi ba yasa ya karaso Suka gaisa,bayani Sa'ad yayi Mai sannan maigadi ya Mai sannu da zuwa,komawa yayi ya bude mishi gate cilla hancin motan shi yayi a cikin gidan,tsaurin karasowa Mai gadi yayi yace Bari nayi mishi magana,dama Aisha na tsaye a wurin window dakin ta Wanda ake it's ganin abinda k faruwa a tsakar gidan,tana ganin shi gaban ta ya bada dummmmm! Cikin ta kohhhh! Ya amsa da kulllllllllll!....................

________________________Knocking Mai gadi yayi a kofan parlor,bayan kaman minti 1 Mai aikin su ta bude,gaisawa sukayi sannan yayi Mata bayanin abin da k tafe da shi,da toh ta amsa sannan ta kulle kofan ta nufi ciki, upstairs ta hau ta nufi dakin hajiya,sallama tayi hajiya ta Bata izinin shiga,shiga tayi da sallama ta suka har kasa ta gaida Hajiya,Amsawa Hajiya tayi da murmushi sannan tace da matsala ne,a'a Hajiya dama Alhaji ne yayi bako,yanzu Mai gadi ya gayamin shine nace Bari inzo in sanar Miki,Dan shiru Hajiya tayi dan,tasan Alhaji Baisan da zuwan wani baki ba,Ina bakon yake,amsawa tayi da inaga Yana waje,ahhhh! Ki kai shi parlorn saukar da Baki na Alhaji,Kuma ki kaimai abinsha da abinci.da toh ta amsa sannan ta mike ta fita,tana fita Hajiya ta mike ta fita daga dakin ta ta nufi na Alhaji,shiga tayi da sallama,samun shi tayi ya kishingida da gado yana zaune akan sallaya,amsa Mata sallan yayi sannan yace lafiya dai kohhhh! Hajiya, murmushi Hajiya tayi tace lafiya Kuma ba lafiya ba,gyara Zama Alhaji yayi da tsauri yace Mai ya faru, murmushi Hajiya tayi tace ka kwantar da hankalin ka ba wani abun bane Dana bako kayi, girgiza Kai Alhaji yayi yace wlh harkin tayar min da hankali, dariya Hajiya tayi tace srry Alhajina,a min afuwa, ta fada tana Kama kunnen ta, murmushi daya fitar da wushiryar shi da ainahin Kamala da mutuncin shi yayi,Yana Mai Jan kumatun Hajiya,dariya duk sukayi suka dunguma zuwa waje,daki Hajiya ta koma while Alhaji ya wuce parlor baki.......

Sallama Alhaji yayi a kofan parlor,kan shi tsaye ya kalli ko waye bakon,hada Ido sukayi da Sa'ad,suna hada Ido Sa'ad ya tsaukar da nashi sannan ya duka harkasa ya gaishe da Alhaji,Amsawa Alhaji yayi Yana Mai Zama akan kujera sannan ya bama Sa'ad izinin Zama,kin Zama akan kushin Sa'ad yayi ya gyara zaman shi akasa Yana Mai tankwashe kafa kaman zaici abinci,tsayawa Alhaji yayi Yana kallan shi Dan Bai Gane fuskan shi ba,Amman yanayin yanda yaga yaron sai ya ji ya birge shi Dan da Alama akwai tarbiya tattare da shi.

Gaishe da shi Sa'ad yayi sannan yayi shiru kan shi a kasa,Amsa mishi Alhaji yayi da kukawa,Bayan Kama minti 5 shiru ya ratsa parlor,Alhaji ne ya katse shirun da Dan tsaurayi Mai k tafe da Kai,Dan shiru Sa'ad yayi ya sunnan da Kai kasa, murmushi Alhaji yayi,kayi magana,karkaji komai kaji."Gyara Zama Sa'ad yayi sannan ya Fara Koro bayanin abin da k tafe da shi,tun da ya Fara magana Alhaji yayi shiru Yana kallan shi har ya Kai Aya,A karshen ko yace Allah yasa ba'ayi Mata Miji ba",shiru Alhaji yayi Yana nazartan yaron da kamalan shi da koman shi,Dan jimmmmmm Alhaji yayi sannan yace a gaskiya dai banyi ma Aisha miji ba,Kuma Ni diyata karatu take a halin Yanzu,bansan na Mata aure har sai ta gama karatun ta,Amman yanayin yanda kazomin da Kuma kwarjinin ka da sharadin da kabi( wato yanda Addini ya yarda a naimi Aure),zan baka izinin naiman Auren ta Amman da sharadiiii............,ya karasa maganar Yana daga yatsar shi daya,Sa'ad najin haka gaban shi ya Fadi,"Sharadin shi ne "zata cigaba da karatun ta",Aiki ne dai ban tilas ta ba,wannan duk yanda kayi dai-dai me tun da Kaine mijin ta Im Allah yaso,"yanda Sa'ad yaji baban na magana ya tabbatar mishi da yasamu karbuwa........"duk a zuciya yake wannan batun,"Dan murmushi Sa'ad yayi sannan yace insha Allah baba zata cigaba,bazan baka kunyya ba", da toh shikenan Alhaji ya amsa sannan yace An baka izinin zuwa naiman ta,Amman indai diyata tace Bata son ka toh tabbas bazan Mata dole ba,Allah yayi Mana jakora, da Ameeen Sa'ad ya amsa,Alhaji ne yace baka Sha komai ba, murmushin da yasa wushiryar shi fitowa yayi sannan yace,na koshi Alhaji,kallan shi Alhaji yayi yace I baka Isa ba sai kasha in ba haka ba baka tafiya,"yanda dattijon k Mai magana Yana birge shi sossai hakan yada hankalin shi ya Kara kwanciya da yarinyar,yasan ko ba komai yayi Aure a gidan mutunci.........Daukan drink daya yayi ya tsiyaya a cup sannan ya shanye.bayan ya shanye Alhaji yace ko Kai fahhh! Yanzu zaka iya tafiya, murmushi Sa'ad yayi Yana Sosa kan shi sannan yace zan tafi Alhaji,wani laida ya dauko ya ajiye a gaban Alhaji yace a kaima hajiya,ahhhh!Mai haka zo ka dauka Dan Allah an gode, girgiza Kai Sa'ad yayi yace ayi hakuri a amsa Abba Dan Allah ba Dan Ni ba,shiru Alhaji yayi yakasa cewa komai sai kallon Sa'ad da yake,Sa'ad ko Jin yayi shiru yasa shi sannin bazai miyar ba,San tafi Alhaji a gaida gida,tashi Abbab yayi yace toh Allah ya kiyaye ka gaishe da baban ka,da toh!Sa'ad ya amsa Yana Mai fita daga parlor,wurin motan shi ya nufa ya shiga yayi Mai key,horn yayi a bakin gate Mai gadi ya bude mai,hannu yasa ya a Aljihun ya dauko kudin da Baisan adadin su ba ya Mika mai,amsa Mai gadi yayi Yana godiya sossai,fita yayi daga gidan Bai zarce ko Ina ba sai gida,ko daya Kai gida 9:30,main parlor ya shiga ya ci abinci, sannan ya Dan taba Hira da mummy har karfe 10:30...........ko daya koma dakin shi wanka ya shiga bayan ya fito ya feshe jikin shi da Tulare sannan ya zura jallabiya ba tare da ya tsaya shafa Mai ba,darduma ya shinfida sannan ya haukai yayi shafa'i da wutri,Bayan ya idar ya Dan Yi wasu Addu'oi,bashi ya kammala ba sai karfe 11.......ko daya tashi linke darduman yayi sannan ya zare jallabiyan ya tsaura daga shi sai gajeran wando,ajiye su yayi sannan ya bi lafiyan gado,wayan shi ya dauka ya danna ma layin ta Kira,sai da Kiran ta kusa katsewa kafin ta daga.

Sallama tayi Mai cikin sanyin muryar ta Mai dadin ji da gardi,amsa Mata yayi da natsuwa,Yana tanbayan ta mutanen gida,daganan daki Hira ta barke a tsakanin su inda duk Suka tsaki jiki suna waya,ba su Suka kammala ba sai karfe 12: 26,Shima Dan suna da wurin zuwa be yasa ya katse hiran.bayan duk sun ajiye wayan Sa'ad ya gyara kwanciyan shi Yana ta tunanin irin karancin da baban Aisha ya Mai, yasan tabbatas in ya aure ta ya auri Yar gidan mutunci da dattako,haka dai yayi ta tunani har barci ya dauke shi..............

A bangaren Aisha ko farin ciki kaman ya kashe ta Dan dazu da Abba k bama Ammin ta labari tana wurin Amman duk busu sani ba.haka dai tayi ta murnar ta da farincikin har tayi barci.

A bangaren Lailah "Mrs Abba",yanzu dai jituwa ta samu bakaman da ba,sai dai har yanzu Taki Bari komai ya shiga tsakanin su inda Shima Bai taba nuna mata komai ba.yanzu har abinci suke ci a kwano daya suyi Wasa,su kwana tare.amman har yanzu takasa sake Mai jiki,inda abin k damun Abba Amman Bai nuna mata ba,itako duk a shirman ta gani take hakan data keyi ne zai sa yaji ya tsane ta ko Kuma bai iya Zama da ita ya sallame ta ta auri "BURIN RANTA"batasan ko duk abin da takeyi a shirman ta bane,Dan shi ya dau ma kan shi alkawarin ba tsaki a tsakanin su mutu ka raba takalmin kaza.haka dai rayuwan ta cigaba da tafiya a gidan wainnan ma'auratan biyun.


A bangaren Sa'ad ko ya Kai kudi nagani inaso da duk abin da ake bukata, inda magabatan shi sukace basusan lokaci sossai, Nan dai aka tattauna inda a ka dayar da bikin Nan da wata daya,i.e sati hudu kachal.


Haka dai rayuwa ta cigaba da tafiya inda a yanzu tsaura sati daya da kwana biyu a daura ma Aisha da Sa'ad Aure,sai soyayya su suke Sha ba Kama hannun yaro,sun saki jiki sossai kowa nasan nuna ma Dan uwan shi kulawa,inda ba maisan bacin Ran Dan uwan shi.gyran jiki ake Mata na mutsanman,dama abinka da kudi ansaki kudi sossai wurin gyara Amarya,kawayen ta da Suka shigo ne Suka katse Mata hiran da take da habibin ta,Hira Suka farayi akan yanda event din bikin zai kasan ce,inda Aisha tace it's walima kawai takeso Dan duk tsauran bidi'an ba damun ta yayi ba,wata ce a cikin su tace matsalana da k kenan malanta,ko kinki ko kin so sai munyi event biyu da walima uku kenan ta karasa tana tanbayan tsauran kawayen nasu,da shewa Suka amsa suba fadin haka abin yake,inda suka tsara komai yanda zai tafi Dan ba isasshen lokaci,sun barshi akan za'ayi reception bayan an daura Aure da Rana sai Kuma dinner da daddare,sai washe gari ayi walima,Aisha najin su komi batace ba Banda murmushin da tayi mutsu.



Yau ta kama Friday/26/03/2022 inda aka shaida Auren Aisha Muhammad sani da Sa'ad  Muhammad Aliyu akan sadaki dubu dari biyu da hamsin lakadan ba ajalan ba,Wanda dubban mutane Suka samu halartan daurin Auran ciki ko harda sarakuna,governs,mayya mayyan en ka suwa ciki da wajen kasar,da dai mayyan mutanen kasa,wato kusoshin kasa,abin ka da daurin Auren mayya,Ana daura Auran aka Fara guda a cikin gida,ana ta kirari na ban girma Dan ko a cikin gidan akwai matan sarakuna.da musalin karfe 2 aka Fara diban mutane zuwa hall din da za'ayi reception,a karshe a ka dauko Amarya da Angon ta inda motocin Abokan Amarya Dana Ango k zagaye da su,ana kaiwa bakin hall din Abokan Amarya Dana Ango Suka fito inda aka bude ma Amarya da Ango kofa,suna fitowa wurin ya dau wani kyalli na musamman,sun sanyya Kaya Mai kalan golding while Abokan Amarya Dana Ango Sukasa fari,jerawa sukayi inda a kasa Amarya da Ango a tsakiya,kida Mai kwantar da hankali da sa natsuwa aka saki,inda Suka Fara shigowa suna Dan taka rawa Amman Banda Amarya da Ango,har suka Kai wurin zaman su,Bayan sun zazzauna aka Fara event inda aka Kira babban Abokin Ango Hydar ne ya fito inda ya bada takaitaccen labarin Ango Bayan ya Gama aka tafa Mai,haka ma Abokiyar Amarya ta fito ta bada labarin Amarya to kuga yanda ake ihu ana tafi,daganan aka gayyaci Abokan Amarya suzo su taka,aka tsakin musu  gida,bayan sun Gama aka Kira Abokan Ango sukayi nasu,sannan en uwan Amarya,Bayan sun Gama aka Kira en uwan Ango,daganan aka Kira jigajigan wato Amarya da Ango,inda Suka fito Suka shiga filin rawan suna Dan takawa ahankali Kai da ganin su kasan ba en chasu bane,Dan Basu iya rawan ba,Sa'ad me ya rike hannun ta Yana juyata yana murmushi,to kuga yanda ake shewa sai wheesul da tafi ake,sai daukan su video da hotuna ake inda anan take wasu Suka watsa shi internet,Nan dai Amarya da Angon ta Suka koma wurin zaman su inda Aka bukacu sukara fitowa Dan su Yanka cake,Sa'ad me ya yanko ya saka Mata a Baki tana kallon shi Yana kallon ta,Kuma duk suna murmushi aka dauki hoton tare da Dan shirt video Mai kyaun gaske,itama tayi kokarin bashi Amman da yake ya fita tsawo takasa kaiwa sai tayi kokarin daga kafa Amman duk da haka,shiko Yana sane ya ki dukawa,tafi kowa ya Fara Yi a wurin akace Ango kayi wani Abu Mana,kafin su Ida rufe bakin su yayi sama da ita,haba zo kuji yanda ake tafi da ihu da shewa,mutane sai tururuwan video da hoto sukeyi Dan ba karamin kyau sukayi ba,saka mishi cake din tayi a Baki inda ya ciza kadan sai ya Bata tsauran taci.bayan angama Yanka cake aka Fara cin abinci inda ake serve your self,wurin karfe biyar aka tashi a wurin inda kowa ya watse.

Da mitsalin karfe shida aka so aka Fara diban jama'a zuwa wani hall din inda za'ayi dinner,sai wuraren 7 Ango da Amarya Suka iso inda suke tsanye da Kaya kalan blue,kawayen Ango da Amarya ko Suka saka sliver,tsaurin yayi kyau sossai,jerawa sukayi Suka shiga cikin hall din har wurin zaman su,MC ne ya Fara gabatar da abinda zaiti kafin aka Fara gudanar da taro da Addu'a,sannan aka bukacu labaran Amarya da Ango,sai aka Fara rawa daganan aka ci abinci,ba su Suka tashi ba sai karfe 10:00 ko da Suka Isa gida 10:26,a gajiye duk suke,Aisha ko tafi kowa gajiya Dan ki take kaman ana karya kasusuwan ta,wanka kawai tayi tayi sallama isha'i da shafa'i da wutri dama Already tayi magrib kafin su tafi.tana idar da sallah tabi lafiyan gado sai barci,wata ce ta shigo dakin tana naiman Aisha Kai da ganin ta kasan jini ya hada ba, wata bace face Nooratu yayar Aisha,amsa Mata akayi da tayi barci,rike haba Nooratu wacce ake Kira da Noor tayi tace dama nasani i,na Gaya ma mum tayi barci,Yarinyar ga da batasan wahala,ta karasa tana tabe Baki sannan ya kulle kofan ta wuce.

Washe garj wurin karfe 2 aka Fara walima,indashi wannan a cikin gida za'ayi na en uwan Amarya me kawai sai abokan Arziki.Sai wurin karfe 4 aka kammala taron inda bayan Amarya tayi sallah aka Fara shirya ta Dan da anyi sallah isha'i za'a kaita gidan ta,ruwan wanka aka hada Mata yakai kala biyar duk na gyaran jiki ne da na Tulare,bayn tagama wankan ta shafa Mai sannan ta zauna aka rangada Mata kwalliya Mai daukan hankali da Ido,bayan angama aka dauko Mata kayanta tasa,tana sawa ta nufi drawer ta dau hijabin ta da darduma ta shinfida tayi sallah dama da Alwala ta,bayan ta idar aka Fara daukan totuna har zuwa karfe 7:45,sallan isha'i tayi,bayan ta idar aka ska Mata Alkebba dayayi matching da kayan ta,sannan aka fito da ita zuwa dakin maman ta,wa'azi Mai rasa jiki akayi Mata,inda mum ce last din yi Mata wa'azi Dan sai da kowa ya Gama,magana mum take Amman muryan ta na cracking Kai daga hi kasan kuka ke cinta,tana kaiwa karshen maganan kuka ya kupce Mata, rungume Aisha tayi tana kuka,Aisha ma kuka take,wasu daga cikin kawayen ta da en dakin ma duk kuka suke,sun Basu tausayi sossai,da kyar aka cire Aisha a jikin mum,yayar  mum ce ta rike ta tana lallashin ta,haka aka janyye nayi,da kyar aka rabasu,kowa abin tausayi ya basu,haka aka janyye Aisha aka sata a mota inda wasu daga cikin mayya Suka rakata da kawayen ta da en uwan ta har gidan miji,bayan sun kaita aka Bata umurnin ta ahiga da kafan dama inda akayi Mata Addu'oi sossai kafin ta ahiga,har dakin ta aka kaita,Zama tayi a gefen gado inda en uwanta Suka sauka Dan ganin gida,Basu fi minti 20 ba Suka juya inda matso,hannu yasa ya daga hulan Alkebban ta,fuskan ta na kasa,hannu yasa ya daga kanta,yaga fuskan ta yayi jajawur,sunan ta ya Kira sannan yace ta kalle shi,yagi idanuwan ta da sukayi jajawur tayi ta Sai da Lailah tayi kwanan uku a Asibiti kafin aka sallameta,inda Tasha ruwa 10 Kuma duka babban laida,bayan sun dawo Abba ya fita ya barta da Zainab a daki,inda Zainab tasa tayi wanka ta gyarata Kuma ta gyara ko Ina,Bayan ta Gama ta zauna kusa da Lailah tana Bata shawarana ta amshi yayan ta a matsayin mijin ta ta daina mishin kallon Yaya face Miji, tunda Wanda ta kishi don shi yayi Auren shi,Lailah najin wannan kalman kuka ya kwace mata,haka dai Zainab ta dinga Bata shawara inda Lailah tayi Alkawarin gyarawa.


               BAYAN SHEKARA DAYA 

Mrs Sa'ad ce duke a toilet sai kwarara amai take kaman zata amayar da kayan cikin ta,rikota Sa'ad yayi ya share Mata bakibda fuska sannan ya dagota Suka koma daki,hijab ya dauka ya saka Mata sannan ya riko ta suka nufi parking space mota ya shiga ya jasu Suka bar gidan,Basu zarce ko Ina ba sai asibiti inda aka bukaci bisarin ta Kuma aka dibi jinin ta,Nan aka shiga lab da shi inda akace Nan da awa daya result zai fito,komawa gida sukayi,bayan kaman awa daya Sa'ad ya dawo ya amshi result, congratulations doctor tayi Mai,inda tace madam na da ciki sauran batanan zaka same su a ciki,dariya Sa'ad yayi Yana ma Allah godiya,hannu yasa a cikin Aljihun shi ya Ciro kudin da Baisan yawan su ba ya Bata a matsayin Albishir,juyawa yayi ya fita a asibitin Bai zarce ko Ina ba sai gida,Yana shiga ya tadda Aysha zaune a parlor tayi dai-dai tana Shan malt,Yana shiga parlor baiyi wata wata ba ya rungume ta Yana murna,shiru Aysha tayi Dan batasan Mai yasa shi murna ba,dagota yayi daga jikin shi ya Fara Mata kiss ta ko Ina,Aysha ko sai dariya take Dan dariya ya Bata,data gaji da abinda yake sai ta rike shi tace Mai ya faru Mai yasa shi farin ciki haka,kalan ta yayi fuskan shi da annashuwa yace Albishirin ki tace goro fari ko ja,tace fari,yace kin kusa Zama uwa wato kina da ciki na tsawon wata biyu da sati uku,dariya tayi tasa hannu biyu tana taba cikin tana kallan shi,Dan itama abin ya Bata mamaki,godiya ta dinga yima Allah  a zuciyar ta tana godemai da kyautar daya Bata,haka suka dinga murnanar su,Sa'ad me ya Kira iyayen nasu biyu ya shaida musu,Dan tunda yayi Aure ya nemi kunyya ya rasa,iyayen matanshi ko kunyan su baiji,sai kace bq Siri kan shi ba.

Haka dai suka cigaba da raining cikin su cikin koshi lafiya, dayake cikin ba Mai laulayi bane sossai yazo Mata da sauki,sai ma wani kyau data Kara,inka ganta zaka zata wata sabuwar Amarya ce saboda haske da cikan da tayi.

A yanzu cikin ta ya shiga wata na biyar inda yanzu ta daina Jin laulayi,lafiya Lau take.




Kukan jariri na jiyo en uwa na ta murnan ta sauka lafiya,"Alhmd!" Kusa Kai nayi na shiga ciki Dan ganin waye,Ina shiga na hango Aysha a kwance an gyara ta alamum ba yanzu ta haihu ba, doctor ne ya bada umurni a Miko jaririn,amso shi yayi ya Mika ma mamar shi,Mika hannu tayi ta amshi jaririn ta,"Niko sai leke nake Dan ganin Mai aka samu,Namiji ne kana ganin shi kaga uban kaman yayi kaki barakallahu Masha Allah" umurnin bashi nono aka bata,itako sai tsoro take Dan Bata manta lokacin da kawarta ta haihu ba da akace ta Bata nono,fuskewa tayi kaman bata jiba, doctor ya Kara maimaitawa Amman kememe,maman ta ne ta maimaita abinda doctor yace,hannu tasa ta Ciro nonon Amman ta kasa ba yaron,anyi maganan duniya Taki, mummmy ko ta fusata sossai Dan har ta kusa Kai Mata Mari maman Sa'ad ta rike Mata hannu,Sa'ad aka Kira Bayan ya shigo aka Mai bayanin komai,ko kunyyan mutanen dakin Bai jiba ya nufi matar shi Yana murna, rungume ta da babyn yayi,sura ma yaron Ido yayi, murmushi kawai yayi,magana yayi Mata akunne yace wife kin iya haihuwa irin wannan babyn haka gaskiya kinyi kokari, murmushi tayi sossai Dan maganan shi yayi Mata dadi,suko sun manta da iyayen su na dakin suka dinga Shan soyayyan su,lallaba ta yayi taba ma baby nono,su mummy da sukaga rashin kunyyan ya Fara yawa sai Suka sabe daya bayan daya, mummmy Sa'ad ko girgiza Kai tayi Dan batasan lokacin daya Zama mara kunya ba.

Kwanan su biyu a Asibiti aka sallame su. Bayan sati daya akayi suna inda yaro yaci sunan "Muhammad za'a dinga Kiran shi da Amin",tari yayi taro anyi rabi na ban girma kowa yaci ya Sha Kuma ya tafi da take away tare da kyauta.



A bangaren su Lailah ko yanzu zaman lafiya ya wanzu inda akwai fahimta Sossai yanzu,ta amshi abunta a matsayin miji,inda yanzu duk ta cire shimen da,ta manta da wani wai shi Sa'ad a rayuwan ta.

Shekaran su daya da wata daya da Aure Amman Babu alamun ciki abin na damun Lailah sossai har ya kaiga Abba ya fahimta inda ya dinga kwantar Mata da hankali,yakecewa wai karya damu komi lokaci ne lokacin samun tane baiyi ba Amman karta damu zata haihu,sai dai kawai ta amsa Mai da toh Allah yasa Amman hankalinta ba a kwance yake ba.

Bayan wata hudu dayin wannan maganan kwasam sai ga ciki ya turo Kai ita Bata taba lura ba,ta daiga ta sauya ta ciko Amman Bata Gane ba,da yake Abba ya Kai wata you Bai gari Baisan da wani Abu ba,Bayan dawowan shi ne ya lura da chanjin daya gani a jikin ta,suna zaune a parlor Bayan sungama cin abinci yace ta shirya zata raka shi wani wuri,tashi tayi ta je ta dauko hijabin ta dawo,fita sukayi a gidan Basu zarce ko Ina ba sai asibiti,mamaki ta dingayi Mai Kuma ya kawo su asibiti,haka dai tarike mamakin ta tanson taga iya  gudun ruwan shi,bayan an shiga da ita yace ayi Mata gwaji, doctor Mai suna Asma'u ta Bata umurnin a haukan gado,Hawa tayi,bayan ta hau aka sata daga gown din ta,Dan tunda ta kunbura ta daina sa riga da skirt ko zani sai dai gown,wani gel aka shafa Mata sannan aka Fara Mata scanning,Nan result ya fito alamun akwai ciki a tattare da ita Kuma ba Sabi bane,juyowa doctor tayi ta kalli Lailah taga ko alamun tasa wani Abu na faruwa batayi ba,Kai da gani kasan saban shiga ne,murmushi doctor tayi tadanyi gwaje gwajen ta tasa ta sauka bayan ta sauka tasa suka zauna Nan tayi musu bayanin abinda ta gani,Lailah najin abinda tace tayi saurin salle ta Dane kan Abba tana murna, doctor ne tace lafiyan ki kuwa kinasan ki Jima babyn ki ciwo me bakisan ba'asan Mai ciki ta dinga dogon motsi ba,natsuwa tayi tace naji doctor insha Allah bazan Kara ba,Bayan kaman minti 30 aka Basu result inda aka tabbatar musu da cikin ya Kai wata biyar da sati uku,bude Baki duk sukayi Dan basuyi tunanin cikin yakai haka ba,tunawa yayi da sanda take damuwa Ashe duk lokacin tana da cikin ma batsani ba,godiya Suka dingayi ma Allah,shawarwari ta Basu Mai kyau.

Haka dai Lailah ta dinga tattalin cikin ta kaman kwai,Bata wani motsi Mai karfi,tunda akasan tana da ciki Husna ta dawo Zama da ita,iko ana tabbatar da cikin ta Kaman an Mata wahayi da kwadayi.

Yau me EDD ta ya cika inda ake da Mata ran haihuwa yau ko gobe,a ranar cikin dare musalin karfe 2,ciwo ya tashe ta daga barci,tun tana jurewa harta kasa tasa hannu ta tashe shi,tashi yayi da tsauri Yana ganin halin da take ciki baiyi wata wata ba ya dau basket Dan dama already a hade yake,hula yasaka Mata akai Dan dama rigan dake jikin ta nada kauri,daukan ta yayi ya nufi mota da ita sannan ya dawo ya dau basket ya tashi Husna Suka nufi Asibiti,tunda Suka Isa aka amshe ta inda ta Fara labor Sabin ya moso sai ta Fara nishi,inko ya lafa barci ya dauke ta,haka ta dinga fama har karfe bakwai dai-dai na tsafe Allah ya tsauketa lafiya inda ta sunkuta diyar ta kyaukyawa Mai Kama da ita sak,sai dai ta dauko hancin baban ta sai abin ya Kara armashi.

Kwanan su daya a Asibiti aka sallame su,zokuga murna gun dangi da Abokan arziki,da ranar suna ya zagayo yarinya taci sunan jawahir inda ake Kiran ta da Fanan.Anci an Sha anyi rabo na ban mamaki.

Yanzu Lailah takai wata uku da haihuwa inda suke lafiya Lau ita da babyn ta,yarinyar ta ko tayi kiba tayi bulbul abunta.


A bangaren su Sa'ad ko yaro yayi wayo,Yana da shekara daya da wata uku,"dama wiyan abun shine daukan ciki da laulayi,Amman da an haihu shikenan ba wiya yaro zai girma.

____________________________ A yanzu Lailah nada Yara uku Kuma duka mata,ta farko jawahir(fanan) sai "twins da Amal da Kuma Amra Ana Kiran su hussaina da hassana",jawahir nada shekara biyar inda twins ke da biyu,Lailah ne ta fito daga bedroom dinta tana ihun sunan jawahir! Jawahir! Jawahir! Sai Kiran take kaman zata fasa gidan,da gudu jawahir ta haura stairs,da take dama tana kallo a down stairs,shiga dakin tayi tace Ammi gani,kallan ta Lailah tayi tace wayayi wannan aikin,Ido jawahir ta zaro tace Ammi bani bane kila twins ne,twins ta kira,Nan ko ta tabbatar tun kafin ta tanbaya,janyosu tayi duka ta rike musu kunne tana ja tace,"banace Inna aje Abu Kar Wanda ya taba ba tare Dana bashi izini bahhhh! Ehhhhhh!"kuka Amal ta fashe da shi tace ehhh! Mommy wayyayi bayi baye baye,Amya ye tace mucha, ta fada da gaurancin su irin na Yara, zo Nan Amra waya aike ki,Nan itama ta fashe da kuka tace "wayyayi Ammi bani bane Amai ne ta eba ta bani cine na cha, ta fada tana goge Ido" Jan kunnan su duka biyu tayi tace toh daga yau duk Wanda ya Kara daukan Abu ba tare da nace ba sai ya sani, kunasan na yanke kunnan Nan ne,da ihu suka amsa da a'a,dai-dai nan daddyn su ya shigo da gudu suka rungume shi suna kuka,lalashin su ya farayi,yace Mai sukayi be Ammin Yara,kaga yaran Nan Abbin nan Dana aje zan Sha anjima shine Suka shanye ba tare da sun tanbaye Ni ba," Ahhhhhhh!twins kunyi laifi,Mai yasa Baku tanbayi Ammi kafin ku dauka ba,duk shiru sukayi suna kallan shi,yace next time don't carry Anything except u ask your mom okh,did u hear me" "amsa mishi sukayi da okh daddy" rungume su yayi ya Mika ma Lailah da jawahir hannu Suka karaso ya hadasu duka ya rungume, Yana sa musu Albarka,(Yaya Abba da family shi kenan).

(Yana da kyau idan yaro yayi Abu a gyara Mai tun Yana Dan kankani,idan aka bari,a kace yaro ne I zai Bari lokaci ne,tohhhhh! Lokacin Nan zai sa ku makaro Dan da wannan halin yaran Nan zai ta shi,Yana da kyau iyaye su kula su tankwara yaro tun Yana Karami basai ya girma ba,Dan idan ya girma bazai tankwaru ba,saboda haka A kula Dan Allah.

Zainab wato kawar Lailah tayi Aure inda aka kaita jos,tanada shekara biyar da Aure tazaran ta da Lailah shekara daya ne, yaranta biyu,mace daya da Namiji,namijin ne babba sunan shi Bashir,sai kanwar shi Mai suna Fatima ana kiranta da teema.

A bangaren Hydar ku ya Auri Raihana wato Yar uwar Lailah,shekaran su uku da Aure yaronta daya Mai suna fauwaz. Twins sister dinta wato Ramlat ma tayi Aure,a Rana daya aka daura musu Aure sai dai ba gari daya aka aje su ba,Raihan na zaune a kaduna inda Ramlat ke Abuja Dan anan mijin ta ke aiki,haihuwan ta na farko ta haifi twins mace daya da Namiji daya(suhail da suhailat ana Kiran su da Hassan da Hussaina,namijin ne babba,yaran duka na Kama da uwarsu, most expecially suhailat kaman maman tayi kaki,komai na maman ta dauko har magana da tafiya da natsuwa da komi). Yaran su nada shekara 2,tazaran Raihan da Ramlat wata days ne,Raihan ta Fara haihuwa sai Ramlat,Kuma tazaran su da Twins din Lailah ma kadan ne,Lailah ta haihu da kwana uku Raihan ta haihu,inda iyayen sukayi shawaran a hada sunan tunda kusa ne,shawaran mijin ta Hydar aka nema inda ya amince dama baida matsala,ansha shagak7 sossai a wannan bikin anci an Sha Kuma an rabar. 

A bangaren su Sa'ad ko suna zaune lafiya kaman tub da Taya,yaran su uku namiji daya Kuma shine babba sai Mata biyu,( Muhammad Amin,Maryam da Nana khadijat) yaran duk sun girma sunyi wayau,Muhammad nada shekara biyar,Maryam nada uku,Nana khadijat nada 2.

Husna ma tayi Aure,shekaranta biyu da Aure da yaronta Dan shekara daya,a yanzu haka ma Bata kasan suna Dubai ita da mijin ta,wani bussiness din shine ya Kai shi kasar,dama ba mazauni bane Yana yawan tafiya in dai ta fiyan Mai tsawo ne zai tafi da matan shi,irin wata days biyu,uku,Amman in sati biyu ko uku ne shi kadai k tafiya.

Lailah ne a labour room tafi awa sha biyu tana Abu daya.a awanta na Sha biyar ta haihu inda ta haifi santalelen yaran ta kato Mai Kama da uban shi sak.tana haihuwan yaran ta kwanta sharap ba alamun nunfashin a tare da ita,Nurse ce ta Fara kula da halin da take ciki,dubata tayi taga Babu alamun nunfashi a tare da ita,shiru tayi Bata ce komi ba daukan kayan aiki tati ta duba ta ta tabbatar da abin da take zargi,Abokan aikin tane suka kula da halin da ake ciki dayake duk sun juta suna kallan yaran,Dan yaran yayi kyau sossai kaman ba Dan Nigeria ba,gashin shi har Ido gashi fari sossai har ya Fara Jaja abinka da iyaye duja farare,ajiye jaririn sukayi suka matso kusa da patient,taimakon gaggawa a ka Fara Bata har sawon awa biyu batayi responding ba,cire Rai sukayi Dan sunyi iya yin su abin ya citura har doctor ma yayi Amman Babu respond kuka nurse din da ta rike babyn take tana rungume da jaririn,Jan farin kyallan yayi ya rufe ta,fitowa nurse Mai rike da jaririn tayi ta Mika ma Abba jaririn jikin ta asake tayi gaba,"Nurse kin bani baby matata fahhh!" shiru ta Mai ko juyowa batayi ba,hawaye kawai take sharewa,shiru Abba yayi yabi bayan ta da kallo alamun tanbaya, Doctor ne ya fito sannan nurses biyu a Bayan shi, doctor ne yace Kai me mijin ta kohhhh!, "Da ehhhh Abba ya amsa, doctor yace kayi hakuri mun rasata,Allah yayi Mata rasuwa,sake jaririn Abba yayi yayi baya zai Fadi saurin taro jaririn Nurse din da ke kusa da doctor tayi, doctor ko ya taro gogan,daukan shi akayi zuwa emergency room,taimakon gaggawa aka bashi,Bayan kaman awa biyu ya Farfado,da sunan Lailah ya
